# MachXO2 fuzz dictionary

Generated by `python3 scripts/machxo2_fuzz_dictionary.py` from each target's `fuzz.v` and its Diamond oracle (`corpus/machxo2/diamond-fuzz/<t>/oracle.json`, see [machxo2-diamond-oracle.md](machxo2-diamond-oracle.md)). Do not hand-edit.

- 2779 targets (LCMXO2-1200HC, TQFP100), 95 groups (name prefix; `re_*` / `pll_*` split one level deeper).
- Columns: source = primitives + `#()` parameters parsed from `fuzz.v`; placed = Diamond component counts; LUTs = distinct LUT equations (`@` xor, `*` and, `+` or, `~` not); regs = non-`#OFF` FF muxes; I/O = pad pin:IO_TYPE/PULLMODE/DRIVE/...; nets/arcs/cls = routed nets, route arcs, distinct wire classes.
